                                                                          Guotai Junan International | Annual Report 2023   101

                                                                                                     Report of the Directors




According to the internal control measures set out in the Connected Transaction Management Policy of the Company,
the responsible business units must ensure that the pricing of the continuing connected transactions follow the pricing
policies which should be the same as, or within the price range of, comparable transactions carried out by independent
third parties in the market (where such transactions are available for references). Relevant business units are also
required to consult and obtain pre-approvals from the relevant departments for each continuing connected transaction
(except for brokerage transactions where standard brokerage rate applies). Each of the legal and compliance, risk
management, finance departments and office of the board of the Company is assigned to review each of the continuing
connected transactions from their respective expertise and responsible aspect with reference to applicable rules and
requirements. The Group regularly monitors transactions between the Group and the GTJA Group as to whether such
transactions fall within the scope of the Master Agreement and the New Master Agreement (as the case may be),
whether the relevant annual caps have nearly been fully utilised, and issues reminders to the responsible business
units where necessary. The Group also conducts sampling tests based on the internal audit plan so as to ensure the
appropriateness and effectiveness of its internal control procedures.

The Directors has engaged the auditor of the Company to report on the abovementioned continuing connected
transactions in accordance with Hong Kong Standard on Assurance Engagement 3000 (Revised) “Assurance
Engagements Other Than Audits or Reviews of Historical Financial Information” and with reference to Practice Note 740
“Auditor’s letter on Continuing Connected Transactions under the Hong Kong Listing Rules” issued by the HKICPA.

The auditor has reported to the Directors in accordance with the aforesaid requirements that the continuing connected
transactions (i) had been approved by the Board; (ii) were in accordance with the pricing policies of the Company; (iii)
were entered into in accordance with the relevant agreements governing such transactions; and (iv) had not exceeded
the relevant annual caps.


Disclosure Pursuant to Rule 13.21 of the Listing Rules
On 21 August 2020, the Company (as guarantor) and Guotai Junan (Hong Kong) Limited, a wholly-owned subsidiary
of the Company (as borrower), entered into several committed facility agreements with certain banks in Hong Kong (as
lender(s)), among which the committed revolving facilities with an aggregate amount of up to HK$2,150,000,000 with
final repayment dates falling 36 months after the dates of respective facility agreements.

On 31 October 2022, the Company (as borrower) entered into several committed facility agreements with certain
banks in Hong Kong (as lender(s)) in relation to the committed revolving facilities with an aggregate amount of up to
HK$1,650,000,000 with final repayment dates falling 36 months after the dates of respective facility agreements. On
15 May 2023, the Company and those banks entered into certain supplemental agreements that, among other things,
the final repayment date of the committed revolving facility under one of those facility agreements has been revised
to 31 October 2023 and the final repayment dates of the committed revolving facilities under the remaining facility
agreements have been revised to the dates falling 12 months after the dates of the respective facility agreements.

On 4 September 2023, the Company (as borrower) entered into several committed facility agreements with certain
banks in Hong Kong (as lender(s)) in relation to the committed revolving facilities with an aggregate amount of up to
HK$1,600,000,000 with the final repayment dates falling 12 months after the dates of respective facility agreements.
102 Guotai Junan International | Annual Report 2023

Report of the Directors




On 22 November 2023, the Company (as borrower) entered into several committed facility agreements with certain
banks in Hong Kong (as lender(s)) in relation to the committed revolving facilities with an aggregate amount of up to
HK$2,150,000,000 with the final repayment dates falling 12 months after the dates of respective facility agreements.

Under the abovementioned facility agreement(s), it will be an event of default if GTJA is not, or ceases (directly or
indirectly) to be the single largest legal and beneficial holder of shares in the Company or does not, or cease to, control
the Company. In case of the occurrence of an event of default, the lender(s) may, by notice to borrower(s), (i) cancel all
or part of the commitment(s); and/or (ii) declare all or part of the loan(s), together with accrued interest, and all other
amounts accrued or outstanding under the facility agreement(s) be immediately due and payable; and/or (iii) declare
that all or part of the loan(s) be payable on demand.

The facility agreements entered on 21 August 2020 had been expired on 21 August 2023 and the facility agreements
entered on 31 October 2022 (and supplemented on 15 May 2023) had been expired on 31 October 2023.


Permitted Indemnity
Subject to the CO and under the Articles of Association, every Director is entitled to be indemnified out of the assets
of the Company against all losses and liabilities which he or she may sustain or incur in or about the execution or
discharge of his or her duties and/or the exercise of his or her powers and/or otherwise in connection with his or her
duties, powers or office. In addition, the Company has maintained appropriate directors’ and officers’ liability insurance
in respect of relevant legal actions against the directors and officers.


Sufficiency of Public Float
Based on information that is publicly available to the Company and within the knowledge of the Directors, at least 25%
of the total number of issued Shares was held by the public throughout the year ended 31 December 2023 and up to
the date of this report.


Corporate Governance
The Company’s corporate governance principles and practices are set out in the “Corporate Governance Report” on
pages 70 to 87 of this report.


Compliance with Laws and Regulations
The major laws and regulations that have a significant impact on the Group’s business include the CO, the SFO, the
Anti-Money Laundering and Counter-Terrorist Financing Ordinance and the Listing Rules. Throughout the year of 2023
and as at the date of this report, as far as the Board and management are aware, there was no material breach of, or
non-compliance with, the applicable laws and regulations by the Group that has a significant impact on its businesses
and operations.
                                                                           Guotai Junan International | Annual Report 2023   103

                                                                                                      Report of the Directors




Environmental, Social and Corporate Responsibility
The Company creates sustainable value for its stakeholders by proactively managing risks deriving from environmental,
social and governance conditions. ESG practices are embedded across all operations of the Group and the Board
recognizes the different needs in economic, environmental and social by various stakeholder groups. In achieving
ESG-related goals, the Company has identified certain ESG areas that present challenges and opportunities for its
businesses to operate in a responsible and sustainable manner.

In 2023, the Group had successfully offset its greenhouse gas emissions generated from its operations in 2022 through
the subscription of carbon credit assets of Verified Carbon Standard forestry project in the Mainland China, achieving
carbon neutrality at operational level for the first time. For further details about the ESG performance of the Company,
please refer to the 2023 ESG Report of the Company.


Auditor
The consolidated financial statements of the Group for the year ended 31 December 2023 have been audited by
KPMG, Certified Public Accountants and Public Interest Entity Auditor registered in accordance with the Accounting
and Financial Reporting Council Ordinance, who will retire, being eligible, and has offered itself for the re-appointment
at the forthcoming AGM.

 Management assesses whether the credit risk of loans          •   understanding and assessing the appropriateness of
 and advances to customers has increased significantly             the established policies and procedures for the ECL
 since their initial recognition and applies a three-stage         assessment including the staging criteria, application

 asset is classified as stage 1 when its credit risk has       •   了解及評估所制定的預期信用損失評估政策及程

 applies when a financial asset is credit-impaired. The loss
 allowance for stage 1 financial assets is measured at an

 stage 2 and stage 3 financial assets is measured at an

 管理層會評估給予客戶的貸款及墊款的信貸風險自初始確

 金融資產歸入第一階段，否則歸入第二或第三階段。第三
 階段適用於信貸減值的金融資產。第一階段金融資產的虧
 損撥備乃按相等於12個月預期信用損失的金額計量。第二
 及第三階段金融資產虧損撥備乃按相等於全期預期信用損
 失的金額計量。

 heavily dependent on the macro-economic environment
 and the Group’s internal credit risk management strategy.
 The loss allowance for loans and advances to customers
 is determined based on estimates whereby management
 takes into consideration market data, historical overdue
 data, historical loss experience and other adjustment
 factors.

applicable, actions taken to eliminate threats or safeguards applied.        項，以 及 在 適 用 的 情 況 下，採 取 行 動 消
                                                                             除威脅或應用防範措施。

From the matters communicated with the Audit Committee, we                   從 與 審 核 委 員 會 溝 通 的 事 項 中，我 們 確

the key audit matters. We describe these matters in our auditor’s            們 在 核 數 師 報 告 中 描 述 這 些 事 項，除 非
report unless law or regulation precludes public disclosure about the        法 律 法 規 不 允 許 公 開 披 露 這 些 事 項，或
matter or when, in extremely rare circumstances, we determine that a         在 極 端 罕 見 的 情 況 下，如 果 合 理 預 期 在
matter should not be communicated in our report because the adverse          我們報告中溝通某事項造成的負面後果
consequences of doing so would reasonably be expected to outweigh            超 過 產 生 的 公 眾 利 益，我 們 決 定 不 應 在
the public interest benefits of such communication.                          報告中溝通該事項。

      The consolidated financial statements include the financial                 綜合財務報表載有截至2023年12月
      statements of the Company and its subsidiaries for the year ended           31日止年度本公司及其附屬公司的
      31 December 2023. A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured         財 務 報 表。附 屬 公 司 為 本 公 司 直 接
      entity), directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control         或 間 接 控 制 的 實 體（包 括 結 構 性 實
      is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable           體）。當 本 集 團 承 受 或 享 有 參 與 被
      returns from its involvement with the investee and has the ability          投資公司業務的可變回報以及能通
      to affect those returns through its power over the investee (i.e.,          過 對 被 投 資 公 司 的 權 力（即 本 集 團
      existing rights that give the Group the current ability to direct the       獲賦予現有能力以主導被投資公司
      relevant activities of the investee).                                       相 關 活 動 的 既 存 權 利）影 響 該 等 回
                                                                                  報時，即取得控制權。

      When the Company has, directly or indirectly, less than a majority          倘本公司直接或間接擁有被投資公
      of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group considers all     司不足構成大多數的投票權或類似
      relevant facts and circumstances in assessing whether it has power          權 利，則 本 集 團 於 評 估 其 是 否 擁 有
      over an investee, including:                                                對被投資公司的權力時會考慮一切
                                                                                  相關事實及情況，包括：

      (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of the        (a)   與被投資公司其他投票權持
            investee;                                                                   有人的合約安排；

      (b)   rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and               (b)   其他合約安排所產生的權利；
                                                                                        及

      (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

      The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for               附屬公司的財務報表乃與本公司於
      the same reporting period as the Company, using consistent                  相同申報期間採納一致的會計政策
      accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries are consolidated           編 製。附 屬 公 司 業 績 自 本 集 團 取 得
      from the date on which the Group obtains control, and continue to           控 制 權 當 日 起 綜 合 入 賬，並 將 一 直
      be consolidated until the date that such control ceases.                    綜合入賬直至該控制權終止當日。

      Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive income             損益及其他全面收益的各組成部分
      are attributed to the owners of the parent of the Group and to the          乃屬本集團母公司擁有人及非控股
      non-controlling interests, even if this results in the non-controlling      權 益 所 有，即 使 此 舉 將 導 致 非 控 股
      interests having a deficit balance. All intra-group assets and              權 益 產 生 虧 絀 餘 額。有 關 本 集 團 成
      liabilities, equity, income, expenses and cash flows relating to            員公司間交易之全部集團內公司間
      transactions between members of the Group are eliminated in full            資 產 及 負 債、權 益、收 入、開 支 及
      on consolidation.                                                           現金流量將於綜合入賬時悉數抵銷。

      accounted for as an equity transaction.                                     失去控制權）將計入股權交易。

2.3 Material accounting policies                                                 2.3 重大會計政策
     Fair value measurement                                                        公平值計量
     The Group measures its financial assets and financial liabilities at          本集團於各報告期末按公平值計量
     fair value through profit or loss, financial assets at fair value through     其按公平值計入損益的金融資產及
     other comprehensive income, debt securities in issue designated at            金 融 負 債、按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面
     fair value through profit or loss and derivative financial instruments,       收 益 的 金 融 資 產、指 定 為 按 公 平 值
     fund investments, debt investments and equity investments at fair             計 入 損 益 之 已 發 行 債 務 證 券、衍 生
     value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value is the price that       金 融 工 具、基 金 投 資、債 務 投 資 及
     would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an      股 本 投 資。公 平 值 為 市 場 參 與 者 於
     orderly transaction between market participants at the measurement            計量日期在有序交易中出售資產所
     date. The fair value measurement is based on the presumption that             收取的價格或轉讓負債所支付的價
     the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place       格。公 平 值 計 量 乃 基 於 出 售 資 產 或
     either in the principal market for the asset or liability, (or in the         轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債主要
     absence of a principal market) in the most advantageous market for            市場或（在無主要市場情況下）最具
     the asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market         優勢的資產或負債市場進行的假設
     must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a              而 作 出。主 要 或 最 具 優 勢 市 場 須 為
     liability is measured using the assumptions that market participants          本 集 團 可 進 入 之 市 場。資 產 或 負 債
     would use when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market           的公平值乃採用市場參與者於資產
     participants act in their economic best interest.                             或 負 債 定 價 時 會 採 用 的 假 設 計 量，
                                                                                   並假定市場參與者以其最佳經濟利
                                                                                   益行事。

     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                  非金融資產的公平值計量須計及市
     account a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits          場參與者通過最大限度使用該資產
     by using the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to            達 致 最 佳 用 途，或 將 該 資 產 出 售 予
     another market participant that would use the asset in its highest            將最大限度使用該資產達致最佳用
     and best use.                                                                 途 的 其 他 市 場 參 與 者，以 產 生 經 濟
                                                                                   效益的能力。

     The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in                   本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備
     the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to              充分數據以供計量公平值的估值方
     measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs          法，以 盡 量 使 用 相 關 可 觀 察 參 數 及
     and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                                盡量減少使用無法觀察參數。

      Property, plant and equipment and depreciation                             物 業、廠 房 及 設 備 與 折 舊
      (continued)                                                               （續）
      Depreciation is calculated on the straight-line basis to write off the     物 業、廠 房 及 設 備 項 目 乃 按 直 線
      cost of each item of property, plant and equipment to its residual         法，就 其 估 計 可 使 用 年 期，撇 銷 成
      value over its estimated useful life. The principal annual rates used      本 至 其 剩 餘 價 值 計 算 折 舊。就 此 而
      for this purpose are as follows:                                           採用的主要年率如下：

      Buildings                                                25-40 years       樓宇            二十五至四十年
      Leasehold improvements                                       3 years       租賃物業裝修             三年
      Office equipment                                             3 years       辦公室設備              三年
      Furniture and fixtures                                       3 years       傢俬及裝置              三年
      Motor vehicles                                               3 years       汽車                 三年

      Where parts of an item of property, plant and equipment have               當 物 業、廠 房 及 設 備 項 目 的 部 分 各
      different useful lives, the cost of that item is allocated on a            有 不 同 可 使 用 年 期，該 項 目 的 成 本
      reasonable basis among the parts and each part is depreciated              按 合 理 基 準 在 各 部 分 中 分 配，而 各

      method are reviewed, and adjusted if appropriate, at least at each         用年期及折舊方法至少於每個財政

      An item of property, plant and equipment including any significant         物 業、廠 房 及 設 備 項 目 以 及 初 始 確
      part initially recognised is derecognised upon disposal or when no         認的任何重大部分於出售或預期使

      Intangible assets (other than goodwill)                                   無形資產（商譽除外）

      may be impaired. The amortisation period and the amortisation             形資產可能出現減值時進行減值評
      method for an intangible asset with a finite useful life are reviewed     估。有 特 定 可 使 用 年 期 的 無 形 資 產
      at least at each financial year end.                                      的攤銷年期及攤銷方法於各財政年
                                                                                度末至少檢討一次。

               through other comprehensive income when those                            面 收 益 除 外。本 集 團 的

               or loss, including on disposal. Impairment losses                        全 面 收 益。當 作 出 該 選

               when represent a return on such investments, continue                    並隨後重新分類至損益，
               to be recognised in profit or loss as other income when                  包 括 於 出 售 時。減 值 虧

2.3 Material accounting policies (continued)                                     2.3 重大會計政策（續）
     Derivative financial instruments                                              衍生金融工具
     The Group’s derivative financial instruments are initially recognised         本集團衍生金融工具於衍生合約訂
     at their fair value on the date on which a derivative contract is             立 日 期 初 步 按 其 公 平 值 確 認，其 後
     entered into and are subsequently remeasured at fair value.                   按公平值再次計量。

     Derivatives are carried as assets when the fair value is positive and         倘 公 平 值 為 正 數，衍 生 工 具 以 資 產
     as liabilities when the fair value is negative. Any gains or losses           入 賬，而 公 平 值 倘 為 負 數，則 以 負
     arising from changes in fair value of derivatives are taken directly to       債 入 賬。衍 生 工 具 的 公 平 值 變 動 產
     statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income.                   生的任何盈虧直接計入損益及其他
                                                                                   全面收益表。

     Whilst the Group enters into a derivative contract for trading                當本集團根據本集團風險管理框架
     purposes or to provide economic hedges under the Group’s risk                 為交易目的或提供經濟對沖而訂立
     management framework, it does not apply hedge accounting.                     衍 生 工 具 合 約 時，並 不 應 用 對 沖 會
                                                                                   計。

     Offsetting of financial instruments                                           抵銷金融工具
     Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the net             倘存在現時可強制執行的法律權利
     amount is reported in the statement of financial position if there is         可 抵 銷 已 確 認 金 額，且 有 意 以 淨 額
     a currently enforceable legal right to offset the recognised amounts          基準結算或同時變現資產及清償
     and there is an intention to settle on a net basis, or to realise the         負 債，則 可 抵 銷 金 融 資 產 及 金 融 負
     assets and settle the liabilities simultaneously.                             債，而淨額於財務狀況表呈報。

     Client trust bank balances                                                    客戶信託銀行結餘
     The Group maintains trust and segregated accounts with authorised             本集團於獲授權金融機構開設信託

     as client trust bank balances under the current assets in the                 項歸類為綜合財務狀況表流動資產
     consolidated statement of financial position as the Group is allowed          項 下 的 客 戶 信 託 銀 行 結 餘，原 因 為
     to retain some or all of the interest income on the clients’ monies           本集團獲准保留客戶款項的部分或
     and recognised corresponding accounts payable to the respective               全 部 利 息 收 入，並 基 於 其 對 客 戶 款
     customers in the current liabilities clients on grounds that it is liable     項的任何損失或挪用負責而確認應
     for any loss or misappropriation of clients’ monies. Under the SFO            付流動負債賬戶內有關客戶的相應
     and the Hong Kong Insurance Ordinance (Cap. 41), the Group is                 款項。根據《證券及期貨條例》及《香
     not allowed to use the clients’ monies to settle its own obligation.          港 保 險 業 條 例》（第41章），本 集 團

      Fiduciary activities                                                       受託活動
      Apart from the client trust bank balances as mentioned above, the          除上文所述的客戶信託銀行結餘
      Group provides brokerage and asset management services and the             外，本 集 團 提 供 經 紀 服 務 及 資 產 管
      Group acts in a fiduciary capacity which results in the holding or         理 服 務，本 集 團 在 當 中 以 受 託 人 身
      placing of assets on behalf of its customers. These assets and any         份 行 事，以 致 須 代 其 客 戶 持 有 或 配
      gains or losses arising thereon are not included in these financial        售 資 產。該 等 資 產 及 其 所 產 生 的 任
      statements as the Group has no contractual rights to these assets          何收益或虧損不會計入該等財務報
      and its gains or losses under fiduciary activities.                        表，原 因 為 本 集 團 對 該 等 資 產 及 其
                                                                                 根據受託活動的收益或虧損概無合
                                                                                 約權利。

      and cash equivalents comprise cash on hand and demand deposits,            金等價物包括手頭現金及活期存

      acquired, less bank overdrafts which are repayable on demand and           短 期 限 到 期，減 須 按 要 求 償 還 的 銀
      form an integral part of the Group’s cash management.                      行 透 支，並 構 成 本 集 團 現 金 管 理 的

      including term deposits, and assets similar in nature to cash, which       括定期存款及與現金性質相似的資
      are not restricted as to use.                                              產），其用途並無限制。

      Provisions                                                                 撥備
      A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or              由於過往發生的事件引致目前出現
      constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is             法 定 或 推 定 責 任，而 該 等 責 任 很 可
      probable that a future outflow of resources will be required to settle     能 導 致 日 後 資 源 流 出 以 履 行 責 任，
      the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be made of the       並 能 夠 可 靠 估 計 責 任 金 額 時，則 確
      amount of the obligation.                                                  認撥備。

      When the effect of discounting is material, the amount recognised          當 有 重 大 貼 現 影 響 時，會 就 預 期 須
      for a provision is the present value at the end of the reporting           用作支付責任的未來開支於報告期
      period of the future expenditures expected to be required to settle        未 的 現 值 確 認 撥 備。因 時 間 流 逝 所
      the obligation. The increase in the discounted present value amount        導 致 折 現 現 值 的 金 額 增 加，會 列 入
      arising from the passage of time is included in finance costs in           損益賬的融資成本。
      profit or loss.

     the reporting period, taking into consideration interpretations and          稅 務 法 例），按 預 期 可 自 稅 務 機 關

      In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are           於 應 用 附 註2所 述 的 本 集 團 會 計 政
      described in note 2, the directors of the Company are required to          策 時，本 公 司 董 事 須 就 從 其 他 來 源
      make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the carrying              不顯而易見的資產及負債賬面值作
      amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent from       出 判 斷、估 計 及 假 設。該 等 估 計 及
      other sources. The estimates and associated assumptions are                相關假設乃基於過往經驗及被視為
      based on historical experience and other factors that are considered       相 關 的 其 他 因 素。實 際 結 果 可 能 與
      to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these estimates.            該等估計有所不同。

      The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an                該等估計及相關假設乃按持續基準
      ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised            審 閱。倘 會 計 估 計 的 修 訂 僅 對 作 出
      in the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects     修 訂 的 期 間 產 生 影 響，則 有 關 修 訂
      only that period or in the period of the revision and future periods if    只 會 在 該 期 間 內 確 認；倘 會 計 估 計
      the revision affects both current and future periods.                      的修訂對現時及未來期間均產生影

4. Operating segment information                                               4. 經營分部資料
      Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent with               經營分部報告的方式按照香港財務
      the internal reporting provided to the Group’s senior executive           報告準則與向本集團高級行政管理
      management and in accordance with HKFRSs. The Group’s                     人 員 提 供 的 內 部 報 告 一 致。本 集 團
      operating businesses are structured and managed separately                的經營業務乃按其營運性質及所提
      according to the nature of their operations and the services they         供 服 務 分 開 組 織 及 管 理。本 集 團 旗
      provide. Each of the Group’s operating segments represents a              下各經營分部均為提供服務的策略
      strategic business unit that offers services which are subject to         業 務 單 位，其 服 務 所 承 受 風 險 及 所
      risks and returns that are different from those of the other operating    獲回報有別於其他經營分部。
      segments.

      December 2023, the executive directors considered that the                的 分 部 資 料 時，執 行 董 事 認 為 財 富
      business relating to wealth management, institutional investor            管 理、機 構 投 資 者 服 務、企 業 融 資
      services, corporate finance services, investment management are           服務及投資管理相關的業務為獨立
      separate reportable segments.                                             可呈報分部。

     Loans and advances are categorised into “excellent”, “fair” and            貸款及墊款分類為「優秀」、「普通」
     “individually impaired”. Excellent refers to exposures which margin        及「個 別 減 值」。優 秀 指 貫 徹 履 行
     obligation are consistently met and the payment of principal and           孖展責任及本金和利息付款並無呆
     interest is not in doubt, collaterals provided are highly liquid and of    賬，且 提 供 的 抵 押 品 具 有 高 流 通 性
     good quality. Fair refers to exposures which principal and interest        及 良 好 品 質 的 風 險。普 通 指 本 金 和
     are fully secured but the quality of the collateral is deteriorating,      利息全部有抵押但抵押品質量正在
     shortfall would be expected when the collateral value continues to         變 差，且 抵 押 品 價 值 持 續 下 跌 時 預
     deteriorate. Individually impaired refers to exposures which loss,         期 錄 得 缺 額 的 風 險。個 別 減 值 指 已
     partial or full, has incurred and with insufficient collateral.            產生部分或悉數虧損且無足夠抵押
                                                                                品的風險。

16. Loans and advances to customers (continued)                                16. 給 予 客 戶 的 貸 款 及 墊 款
                                                                                 （續）

     Margin financing operations                                                  孖展融資業務
     The Group provides customers with margin financing for securities            本集團就證券交易向客戶提供孖展
     transactions, which are secured by customers’ securities held as             融 資，以 客 戶 的 證 券 持 作 抵 押 品 擔
     collateral. The maximum credit limit granted for each customer is            保。每 名 客 戶 獲 授 的 最 高 信 貸 額 度
     based on the customer’s financial background and the quality of              乃以客戶的財務背景及所持相關抵
     related collateral. The Group seeks to maintain strict control over its      押 品 的 質 素 為 基 準。本 集 團 擬 保 持
     outstanding receivables and has a risk management department to              對 其 未 清 償 應 收 款 項 的 嚴 格 控 制，
     monitor credit risks.                                                        並成立了風險管理部門監控信貸風
                                                                                  險。

     Margin loans to the customers are secured by the underlying                  給予客戶的孖展貸款以相關已抵押
     pledged securities, bear interest at a rate with reference to the            證 券 擔 保，按 根 據 港 元 最 優 惠 利 率
     Hong Kong dollar prime rate and are repayable on demand. The                 釐 定 的 利 率 計 息，並 按 要 求 償 還。
     carrying value of margin loans approximates to their fair value. No          孖展貸款的賬面值與其公平值相
     ageing analysis is disclosed as the Group considers an ageing                若。由 於 本 集 團 認 為，鑒 於 孖 展 貸
     analysis does not give additional value in view of the nature of the         款 業 務 的 性 質，賬 齡 分 析 並 不 會 提
     margin loans business. The amount of credit facilities granted to            供 額 外 價 值，故 概 無 披 露 賬 齡 分
     margin clients is determined by the discounted market value of the           析。授 予 孖 展 客 戶 的 信 貸 融 資 額 乃
     collateral securities accepted by the Group. As at 31 December               根據本集團接納的抵押證券貼現市
     2023, the total value of securities pledged as collateral in respect         值 釐 定。於2023年12月31日，作
     of the margin loans was approximately HK$30,655 million (2022:               為孖展貸款抵押品的已抵押證券總
     HK$30,561 million) based on the market value of the securities as            值 約 為30,655百 萬 港 元（ 2022年：
     at the end of the reporting period.                                          30,561百 萬 港 元），此 乃 按 證 券 於
                                                                                  報告期末的市值計算得出。

     Term loans to customers                                                      給予客戶的定期貸款
     The Group also provides term loans to customers. In determining              本 集 團 亦 向 客 戶 提 供 定 期 貸 款。於
     the interest rates, reference is made to the credit standing of              釐 定 利 率 時，會 參 考 相 關 客 戶 的 財
     the relevant customers and the quality and value of the collateral           務信貸狀況以及所質押的抵押品質
     pledged.                                                                     素及價值。

     As at 31 December 2023, all term loans were categorised as Stage             於2023年12月31日，所 有 定 期 貸 款
     3 and were fully impaired. As at 31 December 2022, the term loans            均 分 類 為 第3階 段 並 全 額 減 值。於
     granted to customers amounted to HK$434,122,000, and the                     2022年12月31日，授予客戶的定期
     accrued interest amounted to HK$33,540,000.                                  貸款為數434,122,000港元，應計利

21. Derivative financial instruments                                          21. 衍生金融工具
     The table below shows the fair values of derivative financial                    下表呈列記錄為資產或負債的衍生
     instruments recorded as assets or liabilities together with their                金融工具的公平值連同其名義金
     notional amounts. The notional amount, recorded gross, is the                    額。所 記 錄 的 名 義 金 額 總 額 為 衍 生
     amount of a derivative’s underlying asset, reference rate or index               工 具 相 關 資 產、參 考 匯 率 或 指 數 的
     and is the basis upon which changes in the value of the derivative               金 額，並 為 衍 生 工 具 價 值 計 量 變 動
     are measured. The notional amounts indicate the volume of                        的 基 準。名 義 金 額 表 明 於 年 末 未 完
     transactions outstanding at the year end and are indicative of                   成 的 交 易 量，既 不 是 市 場 風 險 亦 非
     neither the market risk nor the credit risk.                                     信貸風險的指標。

29. Obligations under repurchase agreements                                 29. 回購協議的債項
      The obligations under repurchase agreements arise when the              本 集 團 根 據 回 購 協 議 出 售 證 券，連

      repurchase at a specified later date and price. The amount received     日 期 及 價 格 回 購 證 券。由 於 本 集 團
      by the Group is recognised as liabilities as the Group retains          保 留 證 券 的 絕 大 部 分 風 險 及 回 報，
      substantially all risks and returns of the securities.                  故本集團收取的款項確認為負債。

          The remuneration of the Executive Directors and senior                 執行董事及高級管理人員的
          management is determined by the Remuneration Committee                 薪酬待遇由本公司薪酬委員
          of the Company. The Remuneration Committee, authorized by              會釐定。薪酬委員會受董事會
          the Board, is responsible not only for setting the remuneration        授權，除厘定執行董事及高級
          of the executive directors and senior management but also for          管理人員的薪酬外，亦負責就
          making recommendations to the Board on the remuneration of             非執行董事之薪酬事宜向董
          Non-executive Directors, establishing formal and transparent           事會作出推薦建議、為制訂薪
          procedures for formulating remuneration policies, and                  酬政策設立正式及透明的程
          considering matters related to any share schemes.                      序以及考慮有關任何股份計
                                                                                 劃事宜。

          In determining the remuneration of the Executive Directors and         根據本公司相關薪酬制度，於
          senior management, the Remuneration Committee considers                釐定執行董事及高級管理人員
          factors such as the level of remuneration in comparable                之薪酬時，薪酬委員會考慮的
          companies, the Company’s development strategy, operational             因素包括同類公司薪酬水平、
          conditions, and the results of the performance appraisal of            公司發展戰略、經營情況等因
          the Executive Directors and senior management, ensuring                素，結合執行董事及高級管理
          that remuneration is maintained at a reasonable market level.          人員經營業績考核結果，維持
          This also fully mobilizes the work enthusiasm of the Executive         在市場合理水平，同時充分調
          Directors and senior management with the goal of attracting            動執行董事及高級管理人員
          and retaining high-quality talent to maintain the Company’s            的工作積極性，達到吸引、保
          competitiveness.                                                       留優質人才，保持公司競爭力
                                                                                 的目標。

          The Remuneration Committee currently consists of three                 薪酬委員會目前由三名董事
          Directors, including two Independent Non-executive Directors           組成，包括兩名獨立非執行董
          and one Non-executive Director, ensuring the independence              事及一名非執行董事，確保了

36. Unconsolidated structured entity                                         36. 未綜合結構性實體
     A structured entity is an entity in which voting rights are not the       結構性實體為投票權並非決定控制
     dominant factor in deciding control. Structured entity is generally       權 的 主 導 因 素 的 實 體。結 構 性 實 體
     created to achieve a narrow and well defined objective with               通常為實現有限而明確的目標而
     restrictions around their ongoing activities.                             設，其持續經營活動受到限制。

     Depending on the Group’s power over the activities of the entity          視乎本集團對實體活動的權力及其
     and its exposure to and the ability to influence its own returns, it      受實體回報的影響程度及其影響實
     may consolidate the entity. In other cases, it may sponsor or have        體 回 報 的 能 力 而 定，其 可 能 將 實 體
     an interest in such an entity but does not consolidate it. The Group      綜 合 入 賬。在 其 他 情 況 下，其 可 能
     sponsors an entity when:                                                  資 助 實 體 或 於 實 體 中 擁 有 權 益，但
                                                                               不 將 實 體 綜 合 入 賬。下 列 情 況 屬 本
                                                                               集團贊助一間實體：

     (i)     It is the majority user of the entity;                            (i)     其為該實體的主要用戶；

     (ii)    Its name appears in the name of the entity or on the products     (ii)    其名稱出現在該實體的名稱
             issued by the entity;                                                     或該實體發行的產品上；

     (iii)   It provides implicit or explicit guarantees of the entity’s       (iii)   其為該實體的業績提供隱含
             performance; and                                                          或明確的擔保；

     (iv)    It led the formation of the entity.                               (iv)    其牽頭成立該實體。

     The unconsolidated structured entities invest in a range of asset         未綜合結構性實體投資於多種資產
     classes. The Group receives income from these unconsolidated              類 別。本 集 團 通 過 自 該 等 實 體 發 行
     structured entities through the receipt of arrangement fees and           的計息票據收取安排費用及利息收
     interest income from interest bearing notes issued by these entities.     入而自該等未綜合結構性實體收取
     As of 31 December 2023, the Group held notes of HK$1,153 million          收 入。截 至2023年12月31日，本 集
     (2022: HK$2,359 million), under financial assets held for trading and     團 在 持 作 買 賣 及 投 資 金 融 資 產（附
     investments (note 20), issued by these entities.                          註20 ）項 下 持 有 該 等 實 體 發 行 的 票
                                                                               據1,153百 萬 港 元（ 2022年：2,359
                                                                               百萬港元）。

39. Financial risk management                                                    39. 金融風險管理
      The Group’s activities mainly expose to such financial risks as              本 集 團 的 業 務 主 要 面 臨 信 貸 風 險、
      credit risk, market risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk        市場風險及流動資金風險等金融
      management framework focuses on the analysis, evaluation,                    風 險。本 集 團 整 體 風 險 管 理 框 架 集
      acceptance and monitoring of these risks which are an inevitable             中 於 分 析、評 估、接 受 及 監 控 該 等
      consequence of being in business. The Group’s aim is therefore               於 業 務 過 程 中 不 可 避 免 的 風 險。因
      to achieve an appropriate balance between risk and return and                此，本 集 團 的 目 標 為 適 當 地 平 衡 風
      minimise any potential adverse effect on the Group’s financial               險 與 回 報，並 將 對 本 集 團 財 務 表 現
      performance.                                                                 潛在的任何不良影響減至最低。

      The Group’s risk management policies are approved by the                     本集團的風險管理政策由董事會批
      Board and are designed to identify and analyse these risks, to set           准，旨 在 識 別 及 分 析 上 述 風 險，設
      appropriate risk limits and controls and to monitor the risks and            定適當的風險限額並控制及監控風
      adherence to limits by means of reliable and up-to-date information.         險及使用可靠及先進的資訊以嚴守
      The Board provides written principles for overall risk management.           限 額。董 事 會 就 整 體 風 險 管 理 制 定
      The Group regularly reviews its risk management policies to reflect          書 面 原 則。本 集 團 定 期 檢 討 其 風 險
      any change in the market and clients.                                        管 理 政 策，以 反 映 市 場 及 客 戶 的 任
                                                                                   何變動。

     Market risk                                                                 市場風險
     The Group takes on exposure to market risk, which is the risk               本 集 團 所 面 對 的 市 場 風 險，乃 指 由
     that the fair value or future cash flows of a financial instrument will     於市場價格變動而引致金融工具的
     fluctuate because of changes in market prices. Market risks mainly          公平值或未來現金流量變動的風
     arise from open positions in interest rate, currency and equity             險。市 場 風 險 乃 主 要 因 利 率、貨 幣
     products, all of which are exposed to general and specific market           及 股 權 產 品 等 的 未 平 倉 而 產 生，並
     movements and changes in the level of volatility of market rates or         受到一般及特定的市場變動及市場
     prices such as interest rates, foreign exchange rates and equity            利 率 或 價 格（如 利 率、匯 率 及 股 價）
     prices.                                                                     波幅變動的風險所影響。

     The financial instruments held by the Group mainly exposed                  本集團所持有的金融工具主要面對
     it to interest rate risk and price risk. The market risk mainly             利 率 風 險 及 價 格 風 險。市 場 風 險 主
     includes cash flow interest rate risk and price risk. The Group has         要包括現金流量利率風險及價格
     established policies and procedures for monitoring and controlling          風 險。本 集 團 已 制 定 有 關 政 策 及 程
     the market risk arising from these financial instruments.                   序，以 監 測 及 控 制 該 等 金 融 工 具 所
                                                                                 產生的市場風險。

      customers, accounts receivable, financial assets and liabilities          款、應 收 款 項、按 公 平 值 計 入 損 益
      at fair value through profit or loss, financial assets at fair value      的 金 融 資 產 及 負 債、按 公 平 值 計 入

      repurchase agreements, obligations under repurchase agreements,           券、反 向 回 購 協 議 應 收 款 項、回 購
      client trust bank balances and cash and bank balances. Debt               協 議 的 債 項、客 戶 信 託 銀 行 結 餘 以
      investments usually bear a fixed rate interest and they are managed       及 現 金 與 銀 行 結 餘 有 關。債 務 投 資
      through the strategy of trading them within a short period of time.       通 常 按 固 定 利 率 計 息，通 過 在 短 時
                                                                                期內買賣的戰略對其進行管理。

      Interest on cash at banks will fluctuate at floating rates based          銀行存款利息按根據每日銀行存款
      on daily bank deposit rates. Bank borrowings and margin loans             利 率 計 算 的 浮 動 利 率 而 波 動。本 集
      receivable at variable rates exposed the Group to cash flow interest      團面對以浮息計息的銀行借款及應
      rate risk. Other financial assets and liabilities which are mainly        收孖展貸款款項所產生的現金流量
      carried at amortised cost are either non-interest-bearing or fixed        利 率 風 險。主 要 按 攤 銷 成 本 列 賬 的
      interest-bearing with short term maturities within one year, of which     其他金融資產及負債乃不計息或按
      the interest rate risk is also considered to be minimal. The Group        固 定 利 率 計 息，期 限 為 一 年 以 內，
      mitigates its interest rate risk by monitoring market interest rate       故 其 利 率 風 險 亦 被 認 為 微 乎 其 微。
      movements and revising the interest rates offered to its customers        本集團通過監控市場利率變動並持

      interest rate movements on net interest income. The Group regularly       利 率 風 險，藉 此 限 制 利 率 變 動 對 利

      The Group is exposed to equity securities price risk from equity             本集團因持有權益類衍生工具及基
      derivative instruments and fund investments held by the Group                金 投 資（於 財 務 狀 況 表 分 類 為 按 公

      financial assets and liabilities at fair value through profit or loss.       而 承 受 股 本 證 券 價 格 風 險。價 格 風
      Price risk is the risk of changes in fair value of financial instruments     險指金融工具公平值因波動而出現
      from fluctuations, whether such a change in price is caused by               變 動 的 風 險，無 論 有 關 價 格 變 動 是
      factors specific to the individual instrument or factors affecting all       因個別工具特有的因素或影響市場
      instruments traded in the markets. The Group mitigates its price             上 所 有 買 賣 工 具 的 因 素 所 引 致。本
      risk by performing detailed due diligence analysis of investments            集團通過對投資進行詳細的盡職分
      and dedicated professionals are assigned to oversee and monitor              析 降 低 價 格 風 險，並 指 派 專 業 人 士
      the performance of investments.                                              監督及監控投資表現。

      indices. The analysis is based on the assumption that the equity             乃 以 假 設 股 票 指 數 變 動10%而 其 他

     Foreign currency risk                                                     外匯風險
     Foreign currency risk refers to the risk that movements in foreign        外匯風險指影響本集團財務業績及
     currency exchange rates will affect the Group’s financial results and     其現金流量的外幣匯率變動的風險。
     its cash flows.

     the Group’s transactions which are denominated in currencies other        以港元以外貨幣為單位的交易。
     than the Hong Kong dollars.

     For the leveraged foreign exchange brokerage business, the Group          就 槓 桿 外 匯 經 紀 業 務 而 言，本 集 團
     hedges the majority of the client positions through back-to-back          通過與外部對手方的背對背交易對
     transactions with external counterparties. The Group adopts               沖 大 多 數 客 戶 的 倉 位。本 集 團 對 其
     stringent control over its positions to minimize its exposure to          倉 位 採 納 嚴 格 監 控，以 最 小 化 其 外
     foreign exchange risk.                                                    匯風險。

     The Group’s principal operations are transacted and recorded in           本 集 團 的 主 要 業 務 以 港 元、美 元 及
     Hong Kong dollars, US$ and RMB. The Group is not exposed to               人 民 幣 交 易 及 列 賬。由 於 港 元 與 美
     material foreign exchange risk because HK$ is pegged with US$.            元 掛 鈎，故 本 集 團 並 無 面 臨 重 大 外
     The Group also conducts appropriate hedging activities when it is         匯 風 險。本 集 團 亦 於 其 面 臨 重 大 的
     exposed to material exchange risk in RMB. Other foreign currency          人民幣外匯風險時採取適當的對沖

     treasury and finance department.                                          風險由庫務及財務部每日管理及監

39. Financial risk management (continued)                                      39. 金融風險管理（續）
     Fair values of financial assets and liabilities                             金融資產及負債的公平值
     (continued)                                                                （續）
     Financial instruments measured at fair value (continued)                    按公平值計量的金融工具（續）
     Fair value hierarchy (continued)                                            公平值等級（續）
     (a) Financial instruments in Level 1                                        (a)   第1級金融工具
           The fair values of financial instruments traded in active                   於活躍市場買賣的金融工具
           markets are based on quoted market prices at the end of                     公平值根據報告期末的市場
           the reporting period. A market is regarded as active if quoted              報價計算。活躍的市場指可即
           prices are readily and regularly available from an exchange,                時及定期從交易所、交易商、
           dealer, or broker and those prices represent actual and                     或經紀取得報價的市場，而該
           regularly occurring market transactions on an arm’s length                  等報價反映按公平基準進行
           basis. The quoted market prices used for financial assets and               的實際及定期發生的市場交
           financial liabilities held by the Group are the current bid price           易。本集團所持金融資產及金
           and the current ask price.                                                  融負債所用的市場報價為當
                                                                                       時的買盤價及賣盤價。

     (b)   Financial instruments in Level 2                                      (b)   第2級金融工具
           The fair values of financial instruments that are not traded                並 非 於 活 躍 市 場（例 如 場 外 市
           in an active market (for example, over-the-counter) are                     場）買 賣 的 金 融 工 具 公 平 值 乃
           determined by using valuation techniques. These valuation                   使用估值技術釐定。該等估值
           techniques maximise the use of observable market data where                 技術盡可能採用可觀察市場
           it is available and rely as little as possible on entity specific           數 據（如 有），盡 量 不 依 賴 實
           estimates. If all significant inputs used in the valuation for              體的特定估計。如金融工具估
           financial instruments are observable, the financial instruments             值所用的所有重大參數均為
           are included in Level 2.                                                    可觀察參數，該金融工具列入
                                                                                       第2級。

           If one or more of the significant inputs are not based on                   倘有一個或多個重大參數並
           observable market data, the financial instrument is included in             非以可觀察市場數據為基準，
           Level 3.                                                                    該金融工具列入第3級。

     Capital management                                                      資本管理
     The Group’s objectives for managing capital, which represent a          本集團資本管理的目標概念比財務
     broader concept than the “equity” on the face of the statement of       狀況表的「權益」更為廣闊，即：
     financial position, are:

     •    to comply with the capital requirements under the Hong Kong        •   就本身為持牌法團的附屬公
          Securities and Futures (Financial Resources) Rules for its             司而言，符合香港證券及期貨
          subsidiaries which are licensed corporations;                          （財政資源）規則的資本規定；

     •    to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going            •   保障本集團持續經營的能力，
          concern so that it can continue to provide returns for                 以繼續為股東提供回報及為
          shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders;                      其他持份者提供利益；

     •    to support the Group’s stability and growth; and                   •   維持本集團的穩定及發展；及

     •    to maintain a strong capital base to support the development       •   維持穩固的資本基礎，以支持
          of its business.                                                       其業務的發展。

     The liquid capital is monitored daily by each individual licensed       持牌附屬公司的管理層根據證券及
     subsidiary’s management based on the Securities and Futures             期貨（財政資源）規則每天監控流動
     (Financial Resources) Rules. The required information is filed          資 金，並 按 時 向 證 監 會 提 呈 所 需 資
     with the SFC on a timely basis. The SFC requires each licensed          料。證 監 會 規 定，各 持 牌 法 團 須 保
     corporation to maintain a level of required liquid capital which is     持所需流動資本水平為介乎0.1百萬
     the higher of the floor requirement ranging from HK$0.1 million to      港元至15百萬港元的最低要求及其

     and clients’ margin. All licensed corporations within the Group         兩 者 中 的 較 高 者。於 截 至2023年 及
     complied with their required liquid capital during the years ended      2022年12月31日止年度，本集團旗
